Initial condition for Phase 6:\n    scheduled_6 = float(demands[PHASE_START])\n    calc_start = 469.59 - scheduled_6\n\n    for phase in range(PHASE_START, PHASE_END + 1):\n        scheduled = float(demands[phase])\n\n        start_past_due = max(0.0, calc_start)\n\n        if start_past_due > 0.01:\n            # Try 5 first\n            if (calc_start + scheduled - (20.0 * 5.0)) <= 0:\n                days = 5\n            elif (calc_start + scheduled - (20.0 * 6.0)) <= 0:\n                days = 6\n            else:\n                days = 6\n        else:\n            if scheduled <= 80.0:\n                days = 4\n            else:\n                days = 5\n\n        weekly_capacity = 20.0 * float(days)\n        end_backlog_buffer = calc_start + scheduled - weekly_capacity\n        overtime_hours = 10.0 * max(0.0, float(days) - 4.0)\n\n        row = PlanRow(\n            phase=phase,\n            days_worked=int(days),\n            scheduled_demand=_round2(scheduled),\n            weekly_capacity=_round2(weekly_capacity),\n            start_past_due=_round2(start_past_due),\n            end_backlog_buffer=_round2(end_backlog_buffer),\n            overtime_hours=_round2(overtime_hours),\n        )\n        rows.append(row)\n\n        # Next phase calc_start uses signed end backlog/buffer (not clamped)\n        calc_start = end_backlog_buffer\n\n    return rows\n\n\ndef write_plan_xlsx(rows: List[PlanRow]) -> None:\n    wb = Workbook()\n    ws = wb.active\n    ws.title = \"Plan\"\n\n    headers = [\n        \"Phase\",\n        \"Days Worked\",\n        \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Start of Phase Past Due (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"End of Phase Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Overtime Hours\",\n    ]\n\n    ws.append(headers)\n\n    for r in rows:\n        ws.append(\n            [\n                r.phase,\n                r.days_worked,\n                r.scheduled_demand,\n                r.weekly_capacity,\n                r.start_past_due,\n                r.end_backlog_buffer,\n                r.overtime_hours,\n            ]\n        )\n\n    wb.save(OUTPUT_PLAN_XLSX)\n\n\ndef write_summary_txt(rows: List[PlanRow]) -> None:\n    first_5 = next((r.phase for r in rows if r.days_worked == 5), None)\n    first_4 = next((r.phase for r in rows if r.days_worked == 4), None)\n\n    first_5_s = str(first_5) if first_5 is not None else \"N/A\"\n    first_4_s = str(first_4) if first_4 is not None else \"N/A\"\n\n    # noqa: E501\n        )\n    else:\n        summary = (\n            f\"Capacity steps down to 5 days at Phase {first_5} and to 4 days at Phase {first_4}. \"\n            \"This reflects backlog burn-down and reduced overtime need as phases progress.\"  # 2 sentences\n        )\n\n    # Enforce <=60 words with a conservative trim if needed.\n    words = summary.split()\n    if len(words) > 60:\n        summary = \" \".join(words[:60])\n\n    content = (\n        f\"First_Week_5_Days: {first_5_s}\\n\"\n        f\"First_Week_4_Days: {first_4_s}\\n\"\n        f\"Summary: {summary}\\n\"\n    )\n\n    with open(OUTPUT_SUMMARY_TXT, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(content)\n\n\ndef validate_outputs() -> None:\n    print(\"\\n== Validation: assembly_plan.xlsx ==\")\n    wb = load_workbook(OUTPUT_PLAN_XLSX)\n    if \"Plan\" not in wb.sheetnames:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Expected sheet 'Plan' in {OUTPUT_PLAN_XLSX}, found {wb.sheetnames}\")\n\n    ws = wb[\"Plan\"]\n    rows = list(ws.iter_rows(values_only=True))\n\n    headers = list(rows[0])\n    data = rows[1:]\n\n    print(f\"Number of data rows: {len(data)} (expected 49)\")\n    print(f\"Headers: {headers}\")\n\n    def _fmt_row(tup: Tuple[Any, ...]) -> str:\n        return \", \".join(str(x) for x in tup)\n\n    print(\"\\nFirst 5 rows:\")\n    for r in data[:5]:\n        print(_fmt_row(r))\n\n    print(\"\\nLast 3 rows:\")\n    for r in data[-3:]:\n        print(_fmt_row(r))\n\n    # Initial condition for Phase 6:\n    scheduled_6 = float(demands[PHASE_START])\n    calc_start = 469.59 - scheduled_6\n\n    for phase in range(PHASE_START, PHASE_END + 1):\n        scheduled = float(demands[phase])\n\n        start_past_due = max(0.0, calc_start)\n\n        if start_past_due > 0.01:\n            # Try 5 first\n            if (calc_start + scheduled - (20.0 * 5.0)) <= 0:\n                days = 5\n            elif (calc_start + scheduled - (20.0 * 6.0)) <= 0:\n                days = 6\n            else:\n                days = 6\n        else:\n            if scheduled <= 80.0:\n                days = 4\n            else:\n                days = 5\n\n        weekly_capacity = 20.0 * float(days)\n        end_backlog_buffer = calc_start + scheduled - weekly_capacity\n        overtime_hours = 10.0 * max(0.0, float(days) - 4.0)\n\n        row = PlanRow(\n            phase=phase,\n            days_worked=int(days),\n            scheduled_demand=_round2(scheduled),\n            weekly_capacity=_round2(weekly_capacity),\n            start_past_due=_round2(start_past_due),\n            end_backlog_buffer=_round2(end_backlog_buffer),\n            overtime_hours=_round2(overtime_hours),\n        )\n        rows.append(row)\n\n        # Next phase calc_start uses signed end backlog/buffer (not clamped)\n        calc_start = end_backlog_buffer\n\n    return rows\n\n\ndef write_plan_xlsx(rows: List[PlanRow]) -> None:\n    wb = Workbook()\n    ws = wb.active\n    ws.title = \"Plan\"\n\n    headers = [\n        \"Phase\",\n        \"Days Worked\",\n        \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Start of Phase Past Due (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"End of Phase Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Overtime Hours\",\n    ]\n\n    ws.append(headers)\n\n    for r in rows:\n        ws.append(\n            [\n                r.phase,\n                r.days_worked,\n                r.scheduled_demand,\n                r.weekly_capacity,\n                r.start_past_due,\n                r.end_backlog_buffer,\n                r.overtime_hours,\n            ]\n        )\n\n    wb.save(OUTPUT_PLAN_XLSX)\n\n\ndef write_summary_txt(rows: List[PlanRow]) -> None:\n    first_5 = next((r.phase for r in rows if r.days_worked == 5), None)\n    first_4 = next((r.phase for r in rows if r.days_worked == 4), None)\n\n    first_5_s = str(first_5) if first_5 is not None else \"N/A\"\n    first_4_s = str(first_4) if first_4 is not None else \"N/A\"\n\n    # Manager-facing summary, <=60 words, <=3 sentences, mention both.\n    if first_5 is None and first_4 is None:\n        summary = (\n            \"The plan never steps down to 5 or 4 days (both N/A), \"\n            \"so sustained 6-day capacity is required to manage backlog across phases 6\u0016254.\"\n        )\n    elif first_5 is not None and first_4 is None:\n        summary = (\n            f\"Capacity steps down to 5 days at Phase {first_5}, while 4 days is N/A. \"\n            \"This indicates backlog reduces enough for a lower weekly cadence, but not to a 4-day week within phases 6\u0016254.\"\n        )\n    elif first_5 is None and first_4 is not None:\n        summary = (\n            f\"Capacity reaches a 4-day week at Phase {first_4}, while 5 days is N/A. \"\n            \"This suggests the schedule can be covered without overtime earlier than expected, with demand staying manageable.\"  # noqa: E501\n        )\n    else:\n        summary = (\n            f\"Capacity steps down to 5 days at Phase {first_5} and to 4 days at Phase {first_4}. \"\n            \"This reflects backlog burn-down and reduced overtime need as phases progress.\"  # 2 sentences\n        )\n\n    # Enforce <=60 words with a conservative trim if needed.\n    words = summary.split()\n    if len(words) > 60:\n        summary = \" \".join(words[:60])\n\n    content = (\n        f\"First_Week_5_Days: {first_5_s}\\n\"\n        f\"First_Week_4_Days: {first_4_s}\\n\"\n        f\"Summary: {summary}\\n\"\n    )\n\n    with open(OUTPUT_SUMMARY_TXT, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(content)\n\n\ndef validate_outputs() -> None:\n    print(\"\\n== Validation: assembly_plan.xlsx ==\")\n    wb = load_workbook(OUTPUT_PLAN_XLSX)\n    if \"Plan\" not in wb.sheetnames:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Expected sheet 'Plan' in {OUTPUT_PLAN_XLSX}, found {wb.sheetnames}\")\n\n    ws = wb[\"Plan\"]\n    rows = list(ws.iter_rows(values_only=True))\n\n    headers = list(rows[0])\n    data = rows[1:]\n\n    print(f\"Number of data rows: {len(data)} (expected 49)\")\n    print(f\"Headers: {headers}\")\n\n    def _fmt_row(tup: Tuple[Any, ...]) -> str:\n        return \", \".join(str(x) for x in tup)\n\n    print(\"\\nFirst 5 rows:\")\n    for r in data[:5]:\n        print(_fmt_row(r))\n\n    print(\"\\nLast 3 rows:\")\n    for r in data[-3:]:\n        print(_fmt_row(r))\n\n    #
